### [1] [Electronic Screening as a Design Lever in La-Doped BaTiO$_3$: Coupled Polarization, Dielectric Response, and Optical Anisotropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30210)
*Sikandar Azam,Muhammad Fareed,Qaiser Rafiq,Jan Minar,Abed Alataway*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过第一性原理方法研究La掺杂BaTiO3（12.5% Ba位取代），发现施主掺杂通过增强电子屏蔽、硬化低频极性声子、降低Born有效电荷与极化，同时抑制静态介电响应、改变电子结构（费米能级移向导带、无带隙缺陷态）并减弱光学各向异性，揭示了掺杂诱发的微观耦合机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 施主掺杂可通过改变晶格畸变与电子屏蔽来调控钙钛矿氧化物的铁电、介电、电子和光学性质，但其微观耦合机制尚不清晰，故需系统研究掺杂对这些关联性质的协同影响。

Method: 采用密度泛函理论（DFT）、密度泛函微扰理论（DFPT）以及Berry相极化方法，对12.5% La取代Ba位的BaTiO3模型进行计算，涵盖结构、声子、Born有效电荷、极化、介电响应、电子结构与光学谱分析。

Result: La掺杂引起各向异性晶格收缩，降低四方性和Ti偏心位移，削弱四方铁电相的能量稳定化；极化降低与增强的电子屏蔽和异常Born有效电荷减小相关。声子计算显示弛豫掺杂结构动力学稳定，低频极性模硬化与铁电性减弱一致。高频介电响应中等增加，而静态介电响应因晶格贡献受抑而降低。电子结构显示费米能级移向导带且无带隙中缺陷态；光学谱表现为类Burstein-Moss机制的吸收边移动、近边透明性增强及双折射降低。

Conclusion: 结果揭示施主诱导屏蔽、极性模硬化、动力学电荷降低、极化抑制与光学各向异性减弱之间的微观联系。但结论仅适用于所研究的12.5%组分，需在更多掺杂浓度下计算以确立定量的掺杂趋势。

Abstract: Donor substitution can tune the coupled ferroelectric, dielectric, electronic, and optical properties of perovskite oxides by modifying lattice distortions and electronic screening. We investigate these effects in La-doped BaTiO3 using density functional theory, density-functional perturbation theory, and the Berry-phase formalism for polarization. For a model with 12.5% La substitution at the Ba site, La incorporation induces anisotropic lattice contraction, reduces tetragonality and Ti off-centering, and weakens the energetic stabilization of the tetragonal ferroelectric phase. The accompanying reduction in polarization is associated with enhanced electronic screening and reduced anomalous Born effective charges. Phonon calculations indicate that the relaxed doped structure is dynamically stable, while hardening of a low-frequency polar mode is consistent with weakened ferroelectricity. The high-frequency dielectric response increases moderately, whereas the static dielectric response decreases as the lattice contribution is suppressed. Electronic-structure calculations show a Fermi-level shift toward the conduction band without mid-gap defect states. The optical spectra exhibit an absorption-edge shift consistent with a Burstein-Moss-type mechanism, enhanced near-edge transparency, and reduced birefringence. Together, these results suggest a microscopic connection between donor-induced screening, polar-mode hardening, reduced dynamical charges, polarization suppression, and decreased optical anisotropy. The conclusions apply to the investigated 12.5% composition; calculations at additional concentrations are needed to establish quantitative doping trends.

</details>


### [2] [Domain walls in $R3c$-structured NaNbO$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30318)
*Jack G. Nedell,Craig J. Fennie*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过第一性原理系统研究了钙钛矿氧化物 NaNbO₃ 低温三方 R3c 相中的结构畴壁，分类出八类畴壁，其中一类 109° 畴壁能量极低（9 mJ/m²），与实验观测的晶体孪生相符，并据此提出了 R3c ↔ Pbcm 相变的原子机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 钙钛矿氧化物 NaNbO₃ 的低温 R3c 相中存在结构畴壁，但对其畴壁的系统分类、能量特性以及与实验观测孪生结构、相变机制之间的关系尚不清楚，因此需要开展系统的第一性原理研究。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算，结合群论分类方法，在 R3c 结构中引入八类独特的畴壁（其中四类的取向由弹性兼容性和电荷中性条件唯一确定），并对每类代表性畴壁进行结构弛豫。

Result: 发现八类畴壁中有一类 109° 畴壁能量显著较低，仅为 9 mJ/m²，可与 BaTiO₃ 中的低能铁电畴壁相比拟；且该类 109° 畴壁与 R3c 相实验中观测到的晶体孪生相容。

Conclusion: 这类 109° 畴壁的低能量特性解释了实验中观测到的孪生结构，并据此提出了 R3c ↔ Pbcm 相变的原子尺度机制，为理解 NaNbO₃ 的畴结构与相变行为提供了理论依据。

Abstract: In this work, we undertake a systematic first-principles study of structural domain walls in the low-temperature rhombohedral $R3c$ phase of perovskite oxide NaNbO$_3$. Employing a group-theoretic classification, we introduce eight unique classes of domain walls in the $R3c$ structure, four of which have a domain wall orientation determined uniquely by the conditions of elastic compatibility and charge neutrality, and perform structural relaxations for each representative domain wall class. We find that one class of 109° domain walls has a notably low energy of 9 mJ m$^{-2}$, comparable to the low-energy ferroelectric walls in BaTiO$_3$. Finally, we find that this class of 109° walls is compatible with the crystal twinning observed in experiments on the $R3c$ phase, and propose an atomistic mechanism for the $R3c \leftrightarrow Pbcm$ phase transition.

</details>


### [3] [The often overlooked role of sound in liquid thermal transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31127)
*Kamran Behnia*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文回顾了百年前提出的液体热导率 Bridgman 公式，该公式假设声速与热导率成正比，意味着声子是液体中的热载流子；文章追溯其起源、与流体力学欧拉方程的联系，以及围绕抛物型热方程的悖论及其在量子-经典跨越中的命运。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bridgman 公式虽在经验上取得巨大成功，但其概念意义长期被低估和忽视，甚至未被写入知名凝聚态物理教材。作者希望重新审视该公式，厘清其物理内涵与历史地位。

Method: 采用回顾性综述的方法，梳理 Bridgman 公式的历史起源，分析其与流体力学欧拉方程的数学联系，并讨论抛物型热方程所引出的基础性悖论在量子-经典跨越过程中的演变。

Result: 揭示了 Bridgman 公式蕴含"声子作为液体热载流子"这一被忽略的重要概念，并阐明该公式与欧拉方程之间的内在关联，同时对热传导方程悖论在量子-经典过渡中的表现进行了说明。

Conclusion: Bridgman 公式的物理意义值得重新重视：它指明声子是液体中的主要热载流子，其理论地位与抛物型热方程悖论的联系深化了我们对液体热输运本质的理解。

Abstract: Formulated a hundred years ago, the Bridgman formula for thermal conductivity of liquids postulates a proportionality between sound velocity and thermal conductivity. This designates phonons as heat carriers in a liquid. Its empirical success has been under-appreciated and its conceptual implications overlooked. It is not mentioned in well-known condensed-matter textbook. Here, we review its origin, its links to hydrodynamic Euler equations and to the fundamental paradox surrounding the parabolic heat equation and its fate across the quantum-classical crossover.

</details>


### [4] [Peridynamic Thermo-Mechanical Modeling of Cracking in Tungsten under Fusion-Relevant Heat Fluxes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30356)
*Rinkle Juneja,Pablo Seleson,Samuel Temple Reeve,Justin Weinmeister,Gary Staebler*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种基于近场动力学（peridynamics）断裂力学代码 CabanaPD 的建模与仿真方法，用于预测聚变堆相关热流下钨（一种首选等离子体面材料）的热-机械开裂与损伤。仿真结果与 JUDITH 电子束装置的实验分类吻合良好，可识别热流与基体温度组合下的开裂阈值，为筛选面向等离子体材料并量化其寿命提供途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 等离子体面材料（PFMs）在聚变堆中承受极端热流、高能粒子和强中子辐照，其中极端热流诱发的热-机械损伤是限制 PFM 寿命的关键因素之一。现有方法缺乏对钨在聚变相关热流下开裂与损伤时空演化进行有效预测的能力，因此需要一种能结合实验数据、预测开裂阈值并量化损伤累积的建模与仿真手段。

Method: 作者采用近场动力学（peridynamics）断裂力学代码 CabanaPD，开展部件尺度的热-机械仿真。研究针对循环电子束（e-beam）辐照下的损伤，覆盖基体温度与热流的不同组合工况。仿真由 JUDITH 电子束装置提供的再结晶纯钨实验数据进行标定/信息输入，以预测空间上的裂纹分布与时间上的损伤累积。

Result: 仿真结果与实验分类（experimental classification）吻合良好，并能在不同热流和基体温度范围内识别开裂阈值。该方法可给出裂纹与损伤的空间分布和时间累积的详细信息，为预测部件失效与寿命提供定量依据。

Conclusion: 该建模与仿真框架能有效预测钨在聚变相关热流下的热-机械开裂与损伤，仿真与实验高度一致，并可识别开裂阈值。它提供了一条筛选候选等离子体面材料、量化其在聚变相关条件下寿命的路径，有望用于 PFM 的设计评估与服役寿命预测。

Abstract: Plasma-facing materials (PFMs) are the primary armor materials in fusion reactors, protecting reactor wall components from extreme heat fluxes, energetic particles, and intense neutron irradiation. These harsh conditions induce thermo-mechanical stresses, irradiation damage, and overall degradation in PFMs. Among these, thermo-mechanical damage induced by extreme heat flux is one of the key factors limiting the lifetime of PFMs. We present a modeling and simulation approach for predicting thermo-mechanical cracking and damage in tungsten, a leading PFM candidate, under fusion-reactor-relevant heat fluxes. Specifically, we study damage induced by cyclic electron beam (e-beam) exposure under different combinations of base temperature and heat flux. We carry out component-scale thermo-mechanical simulations using CabanaPD, a peridynamics fracture mechanics code, informed by experimental data for recrystallized pure tungsten from the JUDITH e-beam facility. The simulations compare well with the experimental classification and enable identification of cracking thresholds across a range of heat fluxes and base temperatures. These predictions can provide detailed insights into the spatial distribution and temporal accumulation of cracks and damage, while offering a pathway for screening candidate PFMs and quantifying their lifespan under fusion-relevant conditions.

</details>


### [5] [Microstructural Generative Inverse Models for Additive Manufacturing Digital Twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30404)
*Pablo Luna Falcon,Jason Mayeur,Amirkoushyar Ziabari,Vincent Paquit,Patxi Fernandez-Zelaia*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文利用去噪扩散概率模型（DDPM）合成金属增材制造（AM）的数字孪生取向成像显微组织，并通过定量证据验证生成样本的保真度；同时建立了无分类器引导框架以求解性质—结构、工艺—结构等复杂逆问题，展示了其在认证相关不确定性量化中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金属增材制造虽利于制造复杂结构件，但其工艺—结构—性能关系复杂，导致不确定性与风险，阻碍广泛应用。仅靠实验工作流程量化这些关系耗时且昂贵；ICME框架虽能借助晶体塑性有限元等工具从显微组织预测性能，但需要大量难以获取的数据集，且预测精度依赖真实的AM显微组织表征。因此需要一种能高效合成含复杂形貌与晶体学特征的AM显微组织的生成式深度学习方法。

Method: 采用去噪扩散概率模型（DDPM）合成AM的数字孪生取向成像显微组织，并定量评估生成样本的保真度；进一步构建无分类器引导（classifier-free guidance）框架，用于求解性质到结构、工艺到结构等复杂逆问题。

Result: DDPM能够可靠地合成数字孪生取向成像显微图，且生成样本的保真度得到定量证据支持；无分类器引导框架可有效求解性质—结构与工艺—结构等逆映射问题，并在认证相关的材料不确定性量化问题上展示了潜在应用。

Conclusion: 本研究凸显了基于AI的生成式建模作为计算框架关键组件的潜力，可加速增材制造材料的认证与鉴定（qualification and certification）进程。

Abstract: Metal additive manufacturing (AM) offers substantial advantages to the fabrication of complex structural components, but its widespread adoption is slowed due to complexities in the process-structure-property relations which is the cause for uncertainty and drives risk. Accurately quantifying these complex relations solely via experimental workflows is time consuming and costly. Integrated computational materials and engineering (ICME) frameworks address this challenge by leveraging advanced computational tools, such as crystal plasticity finite element models (CPFEM), to predict critical mechanical properties directly from microstructural data. However, these frameworks require a large datasets that are often difficult to obtain, and their predictive accuracy depends on the availability of realistic AM microstructure representations. Generative deep learning models are a promising approach towards synthesizing AM microstructures which contain complex morphological and crystallographic features. Denoising diffusion probabilistic models (DDPMs), in particular, show a strong potential to capture the inherent complexities of AM structures. In this work, we demonstrate that DDPMs can reliably synthesize digital-twin orientation-imaging micrographs, and we support this claim with quantitative evidence of the fidelity of the generated samples. Additionally, we establish a classifier-free guidance framework for solving complex inverse problems such as property-to-structure and process-to-structure mappings, and we showcase a potential application on a qualification-relevant uncertainty quantification problem. Our work highlights the potential of AI-based generative modeling as a key component of computational frameworks for accelerating AM materials qualification and certification.

</details>


### [6] [Design of Nuclear and Magnetic Scattering Length Density Profiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30416)
*Alexandros Barnasas,Edward J. Stuckey,Alexei A. Vorobiev,Joashaniel F. K. Cooper,Sjoerd Stendahl,Björgvin Hjörvarsson*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过设计非晶 FeCoAlZr 薄膜的散射长度密度（SLD）剖面，展示如何利用中子反射测量增强所需信息，并证明核 SLD 可主要通过成分调控，而磁 SLD 可原则上通过外加磁场独立调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中子反射测量中，样品的散射长度密度（SLD）剖面通常混杂核贡献与磁贡献，难以区分所需信息。本研究旨在探索通过有意识地设计 SLD 剖面来增强中子反射术获取的目标信息，特别是实现核 SLD 与磁 SLD 的独立控制。

Method: 制备四组非晶 Fe_xCo_yAl_8Zr_2 薄膜样品，每组由 AlZr 间隔层与磁性层交替重复十次构成；磁性层成分分别为 Fe80Co10Al8Zr2、Fe70Co20Al8Zr2、Fe60Co30Al8Zr2 和 Fe45Co45Al8Zr2。样品采用 X 射线反射测量与极化中子反射测量进行表征。

Result: 随着 Co 含量增加，FeCoAlZr 合金的核 SLD 从 7.14×10⁻⁶ Å⁻² 近似线性下降至 4.63×10⁻⁶ Å⁻²；而磁 SLD 在整个系列中约为 4.3–4.5×10⁻⁶ Å⁻²，基本保持恒定。表明成分主要调节核 SLD，磁 SLD 则可原则上通过外加磁场独立控制。

Conclusion: 通过成分设计可以主要独立调控核散射长度密度，而磁贡献原则上可由外加磁场另行控制，从而实现对中子反射测量信息的增强，为面向特定信息需求的 SLD 剖面设计提供了实验证明。

Abstract: The scattering length density (SLD) profile of a sample can be deliberately designed to enhance the information of interest obtained from neutron reflectometry. Here, we demonstrate this approach using amorphous $\mathrm{Fe}_{x}\mathrm{Co}_{y}\mathrm{Al}_{8}\mathrm{Zr}_{2}$ thin films. Four samples, each comprising ten repetitions of an AlZr spacer layer and a magnetic layer, were prepared with magnetic-layer compositions of $\mathrm{Fe}_{80}\mathrm{Co}_{10}\mathrm{Al}_{8}\mathrm{Zr}_{2}$, $\mathrm{Fe}_{70}\mathrm{Co}_{20}\mathrm{Al}_{8}\mathrm{Zr}_{2}$, $\mathrm{Fe}_{60}\mathrm{Co}_{30}\mathrm{Al}_{8}\mathrm{Zr}_{2}$, and $\mathrm{Fe}_{45}\mathrm{Co}_{45}\mathrm{Al}_{8}\mathrm{Zr}_{2}$, and characterised using X-ray and polarised neutron reflectometry. Across the series, the nuclear SLD of the FeCoAlZr alloy decreases approximately linearly from $7.14 \times 10^{-6}$ to $4.63 \times 10^{-6}$ $\mathring{\mathrm{A}}^{-2}$ with increasing Co content, while the magnetic SLD is approximately $4.3$-$4.5 \times 10^{-6}$ $\mathring{\mathrm{A}}^{-2}$. These results demonstrate that the nuclear and magnetic contributions can be controlled largely independently, with composition primarily tuning the nuclear SLD and the applied magnetic field offering, in principle, additional control of the magnetic contribution.

</details>


### [7] [Suppression of Grain Boundary Nitrogen Degradation by Boron in Superalloys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30451)
*C. Crabb,G. Topore,J. O. Douglas,M. C. Hardy,M. S. Conroy,D. Dye*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文研究了在Ni/Co基γ/γ′强化高温合金中，微量硼对氧化相关降解的影响。研究发现硼偏析到内部氮化物–基体界面，抑制了Ti/Nb富晶界氮化物的析出与长大，从而减轻环境辅助的脆化降解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高温合金虽能承受喷气发动机最热段的高温，但环境辅助降解会限制其寿命。多晶合金中氮会穿透表面保护性氧化膜，形成致脆的晶界氮化物。硼作为微量添加元素能提高晶界强度，但其在氧化相关降解中的作用尚不明确，因此需要明确硼的作用机制。

Method: 研究者对比了无硼和含硼的Ni/Co基γ/γ′强化高温合金，在800°C空气中氧化100小时后，观察其表面均形成分层状的铬铝氧化物（chromia/alumina）膜。随后采用关联性四维扫描透射电子显微镜（4D S/TEM）与原子探针层析技术（APT），对内部析出颗粒进行表征，识别其为化学复杂B1型氮化物，并揭示颗粒–基体界面处存在硼偏析。

Result: 无硼合金产生了更大、更深且更伸长的Ti/Nb富晶界氮化物；而含硼合金中的氮化物较小、较浅。4D S/TEM和APT确认该颗粒为化学复杂的B1型氮化物，硼在氮化物–基体界面发生偏析，形成核壳偏析结构。

Conclusion: 微量硼通过界面核壳偏析机制，能够控制有害内部反应产物（晶界氮化物）的析出与长大，表明微量元素合金化是一种可调控高温合金内部降解产物、延长其寿命的有效合金策略。

Abstract: Superalloys enable the hottest sections of jet engines to operate at high temperatures, but environmentally assisted degradation limits their lifetime. In polycrystalline alloys, nitrogen penetrates beneath the protective surface oxides, to produce embrittling grain boundary nitrides. Boron is added in trace amounts to improve grain boundary strength, yet its role, if any, in oxidation-related degradation remains unclear. Here we show that boron segregates to an internal nitride--matrix interface in a Ni/Co-base $γ$/$γ^{\prime}$ strengthened superalloy. Both B-free and B-containing alloys develop stratified chromia/alumina scales at 800~$^{\circ}$C in \SI{100}{\hour}, but the B-free variant produces larger, deeper and more elongated Ti/Nb-rich subsurface grain boundary nitrides. Correlative 4D S/TEM and atom probe tomography identify the particle as a chemically complex B1 nitride and reveal interfacial boron segregation. This shows that trace alloying as an alloy strategy can control damaging internal reaction product precipitation and growth by core-shell segregation.

</details>


### [8] [Decoding Composition-Structure-Performance Relations in Sodium Layered Oxide Positive Electrode Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30591)
*Yunyeong Choi,Hasindhi H. Lokuge,Joshua D. Bocarsly,Pieremanuele Canepa*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文系统研究了层状钠过渡金属氧化物Na_xTMO_2的Na/空位有序、层堆叠序和协同Jahn-Teller畸变，通过大规模高通量筛选提出了材料设计规则，并公开了有序分辨数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管层状钠过渡金属氧化物是最有前景的钠离子正极材料之一，但目前仅少数单一过渡金属化合物中，其Na/空位有序、层堆叠序列和协同Jahn-Teller畸变是已知的，缺乏对二元混合过渡金属体系的系统认知，且决定O3/P3相竞争的物理因素尚不明确。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的uMLIPs排名以单一DFT数据库（如AFLOW、Alexandria、OQMD）当作“真值”来度量形成能误差，但各独立DFT数据库彼此并不一致，导致排名与结论可能失真，因此需要厘清数据库分歧对模型评估的影响。

Abstract: Abnormal grain growth is inherently three-dimensional, making 3D tracking of grain evolution essential for understanding its underlying mechanisms. This study combines synchrotron-based diffraction-contrast tomography and phase-contrast tomography to characterize the three-dimensional grain structure and quantify the size and spatial distribution of second-phase particles in the aluminum alloy AA5252. Using an interrupted annealing protocol, we tracked the persistent growth of abnormal grains and the emergence of abnormal growth in a grain initially belonging to the matrix. Although second-phase particles effectively suppressed grain growth in the matrix, variations in local particle density showed no apparent correlation with either the emergence or subsequent rapid growth of abnormal grains. Instead, crystallographic misorientation and, more notably, neighboring grain size were identified as stronger predictors of abnormal growth rate. Matrix grains were consumed more rapidly when they were relatively small and shared high-angle grain boundaries with the growing abnormal grain. The same trends were observed for the grain that underwent a transition from normal to abnormal growth. Remarkably, even before the onset of abnormal growth, this grain was surrounded by smaller matrix grains with exclusively high-angle grain boundaries, while the local particle density was slightly higher than average. These findings suggest that the same microstructural characteristics facilitate both the emergence and persistence of abnormal grain growth, although the mechanism triggering the transition to rapid growth remains unresolved.

Abstract: Local polar distortions, their collective ordering, and their field-driven reversal have been studied extensively in ferroelectric perovskites for decades. Here we identify an unusual behavior in the ferroelectric halide perovskite CsGeBr3 using large-scale molecular dynamics with first-principles-trained interatomic potentials. In its nominally cubic phase the Ge off-centering forms one-dimensional polar chains that extend over at least tens of nanometers and relax on nanosecond timescales, four orders of magnitude slower than in BaTiO3 and PbTiO3, with CsSnBr3 in between. The cubic CsGeBr3 phase is a dynamically disordered state in which oppositely polarized chains flip throughout the crystal, and a long-lived one-dimensional local polarization coexists with zero net polarization. A simple three-dimensional perovskite thus hosts long-ranged one-dimensional polar correlations with remarkably slow dynamics. In the ferroelectric phase the same chains set a one-dimensional switching mechanism, in which the polarization reverses chain by chain.

Abstract: Composition gradients formed during directional solidification are generally considered undesirable in Ni-Mn-Ga magnetic shape-memory alloys. However, these gradients offer a highly effective platform for systematically investigating composition-property relationships under identical growth and heat-treatment conditions. To exploit this approach, one Mn-deficient and two near-stoichiometric Ni-Mn-Ga oligocrystals were grown by optical zone remelting and characterized by X-ray fluorescence mapping, optical microscopy, X-ray diffraction, low-field magnetic measurements, and vibrating-sample magnetometry. The ingots contained elongated grains and axial compositional gradients, primarily associated with the redistribution of Mn and Ga, whereas Ni remained relatively uniformly distributed. The martensitic and premartensitic transformation temperatures generally increased with increasing Mn content. Neither transformation was detected between 10 and 400 K in specimens containing 20.5-22.0 at.% Mn, all of which had $e/a < 7.40$. In contrast, both transformations were observed in the investigated specimens containing at least 22.1 at.% ($e/a \geq 7.46$). This abrupt change may indicate a possible electron-concentration threshold for the occurrence of these transformations. Saturation magnetization increased with Mn content up to 25.8 at.%, reaching 96.9 A m$^2$/kg at 10 K and 71.2 A m$^2$/kg at 300 K. Post-growth annealing increased the transition temperatures and changed the low-field magnetic response. The results indicate that composition-gradient oligocrystals serve as an effective platform for investigating the relationships between composition, structure, and magnetic properties in Ni-Mn-Ga alloys with the same processing history.

</details>

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究一类带加性高斯噪声的非线性随机复制子动力学，揭示维持多样性的最小噪声强度随系统规模按幂律衰减（指数 α 为模型相关量），并通过 (γ, σ) 平面扫描证明不存在真正的二阶相变，提出"路径多重性导致的非线性涨落抑制"是稳定的动力学机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机扰动虽能阻止竞争动力学系统多样性崩溃，但其定量条件与动力学机制仍未被充分刻画。作者旨在量化维持多样性所需的最小噪声、判定该过程是否为相变，并阐明其底层动力学机制。

Method: 在 N 维概率单纯形上构建由非线性指数 γ 与高斯噪声强度 σ 参数化的随机复制子动力学。基线相互作用下数值测量最小噪声 σ_c 的尺度律 σ_c ~ N^(-α)；将相互作用替换为超线性形式观察 α 从约 1.1 升至约 2.0，并与线性稳定性预测 α = (γ+2)/2 对比。扫描 (γ, σ) 平面计算涨落磁化率 χ_H = N Var(H)，分析其峰值随 γ 与 N 的标度行为；同时测量熵弛豫时间 τ_relax(γ)，考察其与 χ_H 的关系。

Result: 最小噪声 σ_c 按幂律 N^(-α) 衰减，超线性替换使 α 由约 1.1 增至约 2.0，符合 α=(γ+2)/2，表明 α 为模型相关量而非普适指数。χ_H 在 γ≈0.7 处峰值约 0.6，高 γ 时被抑制，γ≈1.4 附近无发散；有限尺寸分析显示峰值随 N 无系统性发散，排除真正的二阶相变。τ_relax(γ) 在全 γ 范围几乎恒定（约 2.9 时间单位），而 χ_H 下降六倍，弛豫时间与涨落幅度解耦。

Conclusion: 涨落抑制源于多条多样化路径收敛到相近终态（路径多重性），而非单一通道的加速弛豫。该机制既不同于噪声诱导相变，也不同于平衡态临界性，是此类非平衡随机系统的稳定化模式。

Abstract: Stochastic forcing can prevent diversity collapse in competitive dynamical systems, yet the quantitative conditions and the dynamical mechanism responsible remain poorly characterized. We address both questions in a class of nonlinear stochastic replicator dynamics on the N-dimensional probability simplex, parameterized by a nonlinearity exponent gamma and additive Gaussian noise of intensity sigma. Under the baseline interaction, the minimum noise required to sustain diversity decays as a power law, sigma_c ~ N^(-alpha); replacing the baseline interaction with a superlinear variant shifts alpha from about 1.1 to about 2.0, in agreement with a linear-stability prediction alpha = (gamma+2)/2, identifying alpha as a model-specific quantity rather than a universal index. Scanning the (gamma, sigma) plane reveals a smooth dynamical crossover: the fluctuation susceptibility chi_H = N Var(H) peaks at gamma ~ 0.7 (chi_H ~ 0.6) and is suppressed at high gamma, with no divergence near gamma ~ 1.4; matched finite-size analysis finds no systematic divergence of the peak with N, ruling out a genuine second-order phase transition. The entropy relaxation time tau_relax(gamma) remains approximately constant (about 2.9 time units) across the full range of gamma, even as chi_H decreases sixfold; this decoupling between relaxation timescale and fluctuation amplitude implies that suppression arises from convergence of diverse pathways to similar endpoints rather than from accelerated relaxation along a single channel. These findings identify nonlinear fluctuation suppression via pathway multiplicity -- distinct from both noise-induced transitions and equilibrium criticality -- as the operative stabilization pattern in this class of nonequilibrium stochastic systems.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从有限时长模拟推断热导率时，区分快速的局部能量交换与持续的输运很关键。本文旨在为早期 Green–Kubo 平台提供一个定量的机制解释：弱耦合四次振子链中，共振邻居交换能量后又相干地归还能量，从而改变平台。

Result: 得到能量无关的参考周期 T_lib=7.77ε^{-1/2}（ε 为耦合）；无拟合参数的首阶响应预测第一个负极小值位于 0.952T_lib，深度为独立胞交换系数的 -0.74 倍；链在相同时间尺度到达第一个极小值，但极小值更浅且存在小的后期残余；混沌胞与能量守恒噪声的比较显示出相干归还缺失或被中断的区间。

Conclusion: 这些有限时间结果提供了一个定量基准，可将早期交换平台与随后的关联动力学区分开来，从而有助于更可靠地从有限模拟中推断热导率。

Abstract: Distinguishing rapid local energy exchange from sustained transport matters when thermal conductivity is inferred from finite simulations. In a weakly coupled chain of separable quartic oscillators, we identify a quantitative mechanism that changes an early Green--Kubo plateau: resonant neighbors exchange energy and coherently return it. The pair heat-current integral maps to the classical pendulum trapping response. Quartic homogeneity gives an energy-independent reference period $T_{\rm lib}=7.77ε^{-1/2}$, where $ε$ is the coupling. Without fitted parameters, the leading response predicts a negative first minimum at $0.952T_{\rm lib}$ with depth $-0.74$ times the independent-cell exchange coefficient. Isolated-pair simulations test this reference and resolve finite-coupling corrections. The chain reaches its first minimum on the same time scale, with a shallower minimum and a small later residual. Comparisons with chaotic cells and energy-conserving noise show regimes in which coherent return is absent or interrupted over the measured windows. These finite-time results provide a quantitative reference for separating the early exchange plateau from subsequent correlated dynamics.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文研究了在交互偏好互惠、但个体以有远见的前瞻性优化方式调整状态的场景下，非互易性驱动的稳态概率流与熵产生率如何涌现，并推导出其随规划时标 τ 的渐近标度律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上，非互易相互作用通过破坏细致平衡来维持稳态概率流；但作者追问：当主体的即时协调偏好是互惠的，而状态调整规则由前瞻性优化（远见）决定时，这种概率流和不可逆性是否仍然能够自发产生。这意在区分“交互偏好非互易”与“优化中引入的非对称动力学”两种机制对时间不可逆性的贡献。

Method: 作者构建了一个最小随机线性二次博弈：两个主体控制含噪声的标量状态，在完全信息反馈纳什均衡下最小化各自的贴现成本。优化后的控制律得到一个 Ornstein-Uhlenbeck 过程，其稳态概率流与熵产生率（度量统计时间不可逆性）可精确计算。通过对耦合纳什 Riccati 方程的分析，推导了短视（τ→0）与长视（远见）渐近行为，并用全纳什方程的数值解验证渐近律。

Result: 对于自锚定强度不等的互惠协调，有效漂移非对称性首先在 τ^5 阶出现，归一化熵产生率在 τ→0 时按 τ^8 标度。该抵消源自耦合的纳什 Riccati 方程。对于顺从者-逆反者耦合，归一化熵产生率趋于非零的短视极限，并从该极限按与 τ^2 成比例的量下降。作者还给出了自锚定强度相等的顺从-逆反对的长视极限。

Conclusion: 前瞻性优化能够产生或抑制归一化的稳态不可逆性，具体取决于底层相互作用类型。这表明远见作为一种序参量机制，可以在动力学层面破坏细致平衡，即使交互偏好本身是互惠的。

Abstract: Nonreciprocal interactions can break detailed balance by sustaining stationary probability currents. Here we ask whether such currents can also emerge when the agents' immediate coordination preferences are reciprocal, but their state-adjustment rules are determined by forward-looking optimization (foresight). We study a minimal stochastic linear-quadratic game in which two agents control noisy scalar states and minimize individual discounted costs in a full-information feedback Nash equilibrium. The optimized controls yield an Ornstein-Uhlenbeck process whose stationary probability current and entropy-production rate, measuring statistical time-irreversibility, can be calculated exactly. For reciprocal coordination with unequal self-anchoring strengths, the effective drift asymmetry first appears at order $τ^5$, where $τ$ is the planning timescale, and the normalized entropy production consequently scales as $τ^8$ in the limit $τ\to0$. We derive this cancellation from the coupled Nash Riccati equations. For conformist-contrarian coupling, normalized entropy production approaches a nonzero short-foresight limit and initially decreases from that limit by an amount proportional to $τ^2$. We also determine the long-foresight limit for a conformist-contrarian pair with equal self-anchoring strengths. Numerical solutions of the full Nash equations verify the asymptotic laws and reveal the effect of foresight. Thus, forward-looking optimization can generate or reduce normalized stationary irreversibility, depending on the underlying interaction.

</details>


### [27] [Einstein's theory of Brownian motion: The non-Fickian normal diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30194)
*A. Bhattacharyay*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文指出，爱因斯坦扩散理论中隐藏着对空间依赖扩散系数（非Fick扩散）的推广种子，表明非Fick扩散同样可以属于正常扩散，并探讨了这种"正常非Fick扩散"的起源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清"正常扩散"的定义不应局限于Fick扩散。爱因斯坦最初推导中默认扩散系数为常数（Fick扩散），但其推导本身已隐含着扩散系数随空间变化（非Fick扩散）的可能性，作者希望揭示这一被忽视的理论源头，说明非Fick扩散也能驱动浓度不均匀而非仅使其热弛豫。

Method: 通过重新审视和分析爱因斯坦1905年关于扩散的原始推导，论证其中隐含的推广条件：当扩散系数为空间依赖时，扩散过程仍可为正常扩散，并据此区分Fick扩散与非Fick扩散在正常扩散框架下的角色。

Result: 发现非Fick扩散（空间依赖扩散系数）可以被纳入正常扩散的范畴，它能够产生或驱动浓度不均匀性，而不仅仅是热弛豫消除浓度不均匀；这一推广的可能性早已潜藏于爱因斯坦的原初推导之中。

Conclusion: 正常扩散并不等同于Fick扩散；正常非Fick扩散在爱因斯坦扩散理论中有其理论根源，扩展了人们对正常扩散概念的理解。

Abstract: Einstein's theory of diffusion defines what is considered {\it normal diffusion}. Historically, Einstein considered homogeneous diffusion characterised by a diffusion constant in his original calculations, resulting in what is known as {\it Fickian} diffusion, which thermally relaxes concentration inhomogeneities. However, in the same derivation of Einstein, the seed of generalisation of the theory to {\it non-Fickian} diffusion in the presence of space-dependent diffusivity is hidden. This indicates that non-Fickian diffusion, which can drive concentration inhomogeneities, can also be normal diffusion. The present article discusses the origin of the {\it normal} non-Fickian diffusion in Einstein's theory of diffusion.

Abstract: We determine the site and bond percolation thresholds of all 23,396 $k$-uniform tilings of the plane by regular polygons with $k\le 10$, the complete set according to the known enumeration. The 486 thresholds with $k\le4$ have uncertainties of a few units in the sixth decimal and reproduce the 22 Archimedean reference values (rms deviation 0.71 standard errors) and unpublished bond thresholds of the 20 two-uniform lattices ($χ^2=19.5$ for 20 values); the others have median statistical uncertainties of $2\times10^{-5}$ ($k\le7$) to $7\times10^{-5}$ ($k\ge8$) and no detectable finite-size bias. The vertex-type fractions alone fix the thresholds for $k\ge5$ to about $2\times10^{-3}$ (linear rule fitted on $k\le4$), while tilings with equal composition differ by up to $6\times10^{-3}$ (site) and $2\times10^{-2}$ (bond). In the range of domain sizes covered, descriptors of radius one account for more than half of this difference, and stackings of identical strips have thresholds linear in the fraction of each boundary type. For tilings that differ only by the registry of strips separated by $h$ rows of squares, the bond threshold difference decreases by a factor of 6-8 per row, comparable to the leading momentum-$π$ eigenvalue of the square-band transfer matrix.

Abstract: Active phase separation in living systems is ubiquitous, yet the microscopic origins of the non-equilibrium terms in active field theories remain largely unknown. Here, starting from stochastic mass-conserving reaction-diffusion dynamics of two interacting chemical species, we perform a systematic slow-mode reduction and derive the complete Active Model B+ field theory, with all phenomenological coefficients expressed in terms of microscopic parameters. We identify concentration-dependent diffusivities and asymmetric inter-species chemotactic couplings as the minimal microscopic ingredients generating the genuinely non-equilibrium coefficient responsible for microphase separation, cluster phases, and arrested coarsening. Our framework recovers the equilibrium limit consistently and is validated by stochastic numerical simulations. These results establish a direct, bottom-up link between the architecture of chemical reaction networks and the universality class of their emergent large-scale dynamics.

</details>

*Hugues Meyer*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究如何在有限资源预算下分配资源以最大化随机搜索效率，通过分析一维扩散搜索者在三种资源模型（外力、方向标志、自生化学排斥线索）下的最优资源分配策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多现实搜索过程在有限资源预算下运行，例如具有环境记忆的过程——搜索者沿途留下长期线索并在后续与之交互以引导搜索。如何分配有限预算以最大化搜索效率是一个关键问题，而以往研究常将资源视为无限或仅作为需降低的成本。

Method: 研究一维扩散搜索者，其资源获取受约束，考虑三种资源模型：外部控制器施加外力将搜索者拉向目标；搜索者沿途放置方向标志以将其运动重定向至目标；自生化学排斥线索作为力场。分别在有无关于搜索者当前及过去位置信息（即盲策略与知情策略）的条件下分析最优资源分配。

Result: 三种模型中，当可获得搜索者当前和过去位置信息时，资源使用应集中在空间的窄区域内；而在遵循预定义时间协议的盲策略中，资源应以稳定速率使用，以确保到达目标时资源利用接近其最大容量。此外，具有环境记忆的非马尔可夫过程中的盲策略因沉积线索位置的内在波动而显著降低效率。

Conclusion: 有限资源的最优分配高度依赖于可用信息的类型：知情策略应将资源集中投放在关键空间区域，而盲策略应匀速消耗资源。环境记忆带来的非马尔可夫性与线索位置波动会严重削弱盲策略的效率。

Abstract: Improving the efficiency of a stochastic search often requires to provide the searching agent with resources that modify its dynamics and help it explore space more quickly. Although such resources are often assumed to be unlimited and treated as a cost to mitigate, many realistic search processes operate under a finite resource budget, e.g. in processes with environmental memory in which the searcher deposits long-lasting cues along its path that it can interact with at later times to guide its further search. Here, we address the question of how to allocate a limited budget to maximize search efficiency by investigating a one-dimensional diffusing agent whose access to resources is constrained. We consider three resource models, namely an external force applied by an external controller to pull the searcher towards the target, directional flags deposited by the searcher along its path that redirect its motion towards the target, and self-generated chemorepulsive cues that act as a force field. For all these models, we find that resource usage should be concentrated in narrow regions of space when information about the searcher's current and past positions is available. In contrast, in blind strategies where a predefined time protocol is followed, resources should be used at a steady rate to ensure it to be used at most of its capacity by the time it reaches the target. We further show that blind strategies in non-Markovian processes with environmental memory are significantly less efficient due to the inherent fluctuations in the locations of deposited cues.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索无需外磁场的可重构超导非互易性（超导二极管效应），并厘清其物理起源是磁性机制还是电子近邻效应，同时研究磁畴结构对磁通流动稳定性的调控。

Conclusion: 条纹畴铁磁覆盖层为NbN基异质结构中的超导非互易性和磁通流动稳定性提供了一种可重构的调控途径，该效应源于剩余磁畴景观中的非对称涡旋输运。

Abstract: We demonstrate reconfigurable, field-free superconducting diode behavior in NbN/FePt and NbN/AlN/FePt heterostructures with remanent stripe domains in the FePt layer. NbN films 10 nm thick are capped either directly with FePt or through a thin AlN spacer, which reduces electronic proximity effects while preserving magnetic coupling through stray fields. At zero applied magnetic field, the superconducting diode efficiency reaches approximately 50--60% in NbN/FePt when the stripes are perpendicular to the current and is strongly reduced when they are parallel. The diode effect persists in NbN/AlN/FePt with efficiencies reaching approximately 35--40%, supporting a magnetic origin of the nonreciprocity. We interpret the response as asymmetric vortex transport in the remanent magnetic landscape, with preferential edge vorticity and a Lorentz force that favors inward flux injection or outward motion depending on current polarity. Under applied perpendicular magnetic fields, the interplay between the external field and the magnetic stray fields modifies the flux-flow instability and the apparent instability velocity inferred from transport, with values below those reported for bare NbN films. These results show that stripe-domain ferromagnetic overlayers provide a reconfigurable route to control superconducting nonreciprocity and flux-flow stability in NbN-based heterostructures.

</details>


### [34] [Cluster property and entanglement in the superconducting state](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30581)
*Katsuhiko Higuchi,Masahiko Higuchi*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文给出超导态满足团簇性质（cluster property）的条件，据此证明用于导出超导态序参量（OPSS）时所用的团簇性质成立，并确认OPSS可表示为超导态下两点场算符乘积的期望值，进而回应了Leggett等人关于粒子数涨落的长期疑虑；同时借助BCS基态最大geminal构成的两粒子纠缠分析，验证了偏离团簇性质条件越大则纠缠越大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决超导态中粒子数涨落带来的长期争议，特别是Leggett等人对常用超导态序参量（OPSS）正当性的质疑，为导出OPSS时所依赖的团簇性质提供严格的成立条件与理论依据。

Method: 首先推导并给出超导态满足团簇性质的条件；在此条件下证明导出OPSS所用的团簇性质成立，从而将OPSS表示为 ${}_S\langle Ψ| \hatψ(x)\hatψ(x') | Ψ\rangle_S$；进一步通过计算BCS基态最大geminal所构成两粒子间的纠缠（基于文献J. Phys. Commun. 5, 095003），考察偏离团簇性质条件程度与纠缠大小之间的关系。

Result: 证明了团簇性质在超导态下成立，OPSS可严格表示为 ${}_S\langle Ψ| \hatψ(x)\hatψ(x') | Ψ\rangle_S$，消除了粒子数涨落对序参量定义的困扰；同时发现偏离团簇性质条件越大，两粒子纠缠越大，从而验证了团簇性质条件的有效性以及常用OPSS的合理性。

Conclusion: 本文为超导态序参量的常用定义提供了严格的理论保障，解决了Leggett等人关于粒子数涨落的争议；并通过纠缠随偏离程度增大而增强的现象，双重印证了所提团簇性质条件的有效性与OPSS的正当性。

Abstract: We present the conditions for satisfying the cluster property in the superconducting state. Using these conditions, we show that the cluster property employed when deriving the order parameter of the superconducting state (OPSS) holds true, and that the OPSS is given by ${}_S\langle Ψ| \hatψ(x)\hatψ(x') | Ψ\rangle_S$, where $|Ψ\rangle_S$ is the superconducting state, and where $\hatψ(x)$ is the field operator of superconducting electrons. This provides a resolution to the long-standing concern raised by Leggett et al.\ regarding particle-number fluctuation in the superconducting state. In addition, by considering the entanglement between two particles composed of the maximum geminal of the BCS ground state [J.\ Phys. Commun. 5, 095003 (2021)], it is confirmed that the larger the deviation from the conditions for satisfying the cluster property becomes, the greater the magnitude of the entanglement becomes. This shows not only the effectiveness of the conditions for satisfying the cluster property but also the justification of the OPSS that has been commonly used.

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于正则化Hartree–Fock的单返回（one effective return）构造，在无需拟合参数的情况下，从HF信息载体出发定量复现Be-like序列、十电子分子与Ne-like离子的标量关联能，验证了“源强度”与“返回限制”的物理可分离性。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 该无参数的源—返回构造定量复现了所建立的分子与离子关联趋势，与已知结果高度吻合；数值一致因此被视为对“源强度与返回限制之间存在物理分离”的验证，而非研究目标本身。

Conclusion: 定态HF信息载体中的信息足以通过一次有效返回来确定标量关联能；无参数构造的定量准确度表明，关联能可被物理地分解为源强度与返回限制两部分，这种可分离性具有物理实在性。

Abstract: A stationary many-electron eigenstate has one dynamical phase although its internal response can be strongly multimode. We ask whether the stationary Hartree--Fock information carrier contains enough information to determine the scalar correlation energy through one effective return. In regularized Hartree--Fock, Gaussian primitive products fix a minimum-information relative-coordinate mode before any one-mode reduction; virial completion defines the correlation source, while exact contact measures the additional return restriction created by neighboring occupied pairs. The Be-like $1s^22s^2$ sequence establishes the one-valence-pair boundary: with no neighboring occupied valence pair, the source returns through the universal floor $D_0=\sqrt{1+δ^2}$. Ten-electron molecules and Ne-like ions then test how finite-pair contact dresses that return. The same parameter-free source--return construction reproduces the established molecular and ionic correlation trends with quantitative accuracy. The numerical agreement therefore serves as validation of a physical separation between source strength and return restriction, rather than as the objective by itself.

</details>

We treat weak positional disorder using the self-consistent Born approximation and numerical disorder averaging, and study independently distributed vacancies using transfer-matrix and multifractal scaling. The local Chern marker and current textures track the transition in real space. Together, these results highlight the role that nonperturbative ring states have as microscopic degrees of freedom through which strong disorder destroys the Chern phase.

We show that the phases of these systems are controlled by the competition between measurements, which favor the area-law phase and particle tunneling in and out of the Luttinger liquid, which favors the volume-law phase. The volume-law phase in this system is always charge fuzzy. We show that within this framework, the phase boundary between the two phases is given by a universal dependence of the Luttinger parameter on the measurement rate. This behavior can be contrasted with the non-universal phase boundaries in more generic highly excited one dimensional systems whose behavior is no longer captured by the Luttinger liquid framework.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为FRIO的量子优化算法，通过利用连续反向量子退火穿越多体局域化（MBL）转变，并借助Landau-Zener跃迁密度加速退火过程。该算法使退火时间随系统规模从多项式量级降至常数级，且可被Trotter分解为步数适中的量子电路，在求解Sherrington-Kirkpatrick自旋玻璃基态问题上几乎始终优于预优化角度的QAOA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多经典优化问题可映射为自旋玻璃基态的近似求解，而自旋玻璃是研究多体局域化的经典模型。作者希望借助量子统计力学的直觉来指导构建新型量子优化算法，以克服传统QAOA等浅层变分算法在寻找基态时的局限，并大幅缩短所需退火时间。

Method: 作者研究连续反向量子退火在穿越多体局域化转变时的行为，通过系统淬火过程中Landau-Zener交叉密度来刻画并加速退火；将成功概率的演化解释为有效Fokker-Planck方程，据此推导退火时间随系统规模N从O(poly(N))降至O(1)的改进（针对能量密度非零的参考态）。连续动力学可被Trotter分解为随系统规模增大仍保持适度步数的离散电路，并提出用热化动力学有效确定含时哈密顿量参数的定量策略，最终得到数字化算法FRIO。

Result: 数值结果表明，在求解SK模型自旋玻璃基态问题时，采用热化动力学高效选取的电路参数，FRIO几乎总是优于使用预优化角度的常规QAOA ansatz。

Conclusion: 通过穿越多体局域化转变并利用Landau-Zener交叉密度，连续反向量子退火可实现高效加速，使退火时间与系统规模无关；所得到的可Trotter化的FRIO电路算法在自旋玻璃基态求解上优于QAOA，且参数可高效确定，适合近期量子设备。

Abstract: The promise of efficiently solving classical optimisation problems on quantum computers is tantalizing. As many such problems can be mapped to approximating the ground state of spin-glasses, paradigmatic models for studying many-body localisation, it is natural to ask if some of the intuition coming from quantum statistical mechanics can help guide the construction of novel quantum optimisation algorithms. In this work, we discuss how continuous reverse quantum annealing crossing a many-body localisation transition can be accelerated by considering the density of Landau-Zener crossings as the system is quenched. We provide an interpretation of this behaviour in terms of an effective Fokker-Planck equation for the success probability of the algorithm. This interpretation suggests an improvement in the required annealing time as a function of the system size $N$, which goes from $\mathcal{O}({\rm poly}(N))$ to $\mathcal{O}(1)$ for reference states with non-vanishing energy density. We show that the continuous dynamics can be Trotterised with a number of steps that remains modest as system size increases, making it appealing for near-term devices. For the resulting circuit implementation, we provide a quantitative strategy for determining the parameters of the time-dependent Hamiltonian. We compare the performance of our digitised algorithm, which we call FRIO, against that of the QAOA for finding the ground state of spin-glass problems, in particular the SK model. The numerical results suggest that with suitably selected circuit parameters which can be efficiently found using thermalizing dynamics, FRIO almost always outperforms the conventional QAOA ansatz with pre-optimized angles.

</details>

Method: 提出一种对称性保持的局域表示框架，通过边界钝化（boundary passivation）严格强制执行拓扑连续性和平移不变性；无需显式周期扩展即可捕获覆盖度依赖的多体相互作用，并自主解耦调制局域反应性的非线性配体效应。

Conclusion: 该方法为理解复杂凝聚态体系中的短程有序提供了一个物理上透明的新范式，在保证对称性与物理准确性的前提下显著提升了计算效率。

Abstract: Modeling short-range interactions in highly disordered materials remains challenging due to the immense configuration space and the disruption of local symmetry in truncated theoretical models. Here, we propose a symmetry-preserving local representation framework that strictly enforces topological continuity and translational invariance via boundary passivation. Applied to high-entropy alloys, this approach autonomously unravels the nonlinear ligand effects modulating local reactivity and accurately captures coverage-dependent many-body interactions without explicit periodic expansion. By resolving the conflict between localized chemical environments and extended periodic boundaries, this framework not only reduces computational complexity by an order of magnitude but also provides a physically transparent paradigm for deciphering short-range order in complex condensed matter systems.

</details>

*Arsineh Apelian,Tanay Tak,Xuance Jiang,Tsung-Yin Tsai,James S. Speck,Vojtech Vlcek*

Abstract: Disorder can localize valence- and conduction-band states to different degrees, yet whether such localization contrast has observable consequences remains poorly understood. Here, we show that localization contrast strongly impacts competing carrier-recombination channels. We illustrate this behavior in random In$_x$Ga$_{1-x}$N alloys. Increasing the In content from $x=13.5\%$ to $x=22\%$ progressively localizes the valence-band maximum state while leaving the conduction-band minimum state comparatively delocalized. This contrasting localization decreases the interband optical momentum matrix element underlying radiative recombination. For Auger-Meitner recombination, however, localization alone does not determine the coupling; atomic-scale disorder also breaks primitive-cell translational symmetry and modifies the momentum-space and phase structure of the states. This generates additional low-$Q$ components and enhances the Auger coupling by more than five orders of magnitude relative to pristine GaN. Thus, we provide a microscopic explanation for the competing trends in radiative and Auger recombination. More broadly, our results establish localization contrast as a mechanism through which disorder can control optical and carrier-carrier interactions in semiconductors.

</details>

### [42] [Discrete time liquids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30266)
*Zuo Wang,Paul M. Schindler,Marin Bukov,Marios H. Michael*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出"离散时间液体"这一新概念：在有限温度腔耦合的自旋系统中，系统呈现短程时间相干的周期倍增动力学，个体轨迹会在两个时间构型间随机切换，这种切换由"时间孤子"（时间序中的拓扑缺陷）引起，破坏长程时间相干，使系统表现出类似液体的时空序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散时间晶体自发破缺周期驱动的时间平移对称性，作者希望探索除时间晶体外是否还存在其他类型的时序多体序（temporal many-body order），特别是短程时间相干的时间序形态。

Method: 研究一个与有限温度腔耦合的二维自旋系统，腔涨落提供噪声的、仅具有限时间相干的近似周期驱动；通过分析个体轨迹在两种对称相关时间构型间的随机切换，将切换事件识别为时间孤子（拓扑缺陷），并提出一个能重现多体系统时间关联分布的最小模型，将时间液体描述为具有有限时间孤子密度的时间晶体。

Result: 在广泛参数区间内，自旋形成具有稳健周期倍增动力学的亚稳态，但该状态仅具有限时间相干：轨迹在两种时间构型间随机切换。这些"时间孤子"保持局域周期倍增，却破坏长程时间相干，从而产生时间液体态。由于孤子可发生在任意时刻，系综平均恢复连续时间平移对称性。

Conclusion: 离散时间液体是时间晶体中引入有限密度时间孤子所形成的新时空序，具有短程时间相干性；这一发现提出了离散时间液体可能在热平衡中存在的有趣可能性。

Abstract: Discrete time crystals exhibit robust subharmonic oscillations, spontaneously breaking the discrete time-translation symmetry of a periodic drive. Since their emergence, the question of whether other types of temporal many-body order can exist has attracted considerable attention. Here we identify discrete time liquids -- a new form of spatiotemporal order characterized by short-range temporal coherence as a result of time-solitons. We study a two-dimensional spin system coupled to a finite-temperature cavity, whose fluctuations provide a noisy, approximately periodic drive with only finite temporal coherence. Remarkably, the spins nevertheless form a metastable state with robust period-doubled dynamics over an extended parameter regime. Unlike a time crystal, this state has only finite temporal coherence: individual trajectories stochastically switch between the two symmetry-related temporal configurations of the period-doubled response. We identify these switches as time-solitons: topological defects in temporal order that preserve local period doubling while destroying long-range temporal coherence, thereby producing the time liquid state. We propose a minimal model which reproduces the distribution of temporal correlations of the many-body system, suggesting a general description of the time liquid as a time crystal with a finite density of time-solitons. Because these solitons can occur at arbitrary times, ensemble averaging restores continuous time-translation symmetry, raising the intriguing possibility that discrete time liquids can exist in thermal equilibrium.

In 2D, the accuracy of the transformed mean-field solutions depends on the site ordering and boundary conditions, as nonlocal strings can appear. Abstract: The auxiliary master equation approach (AMEA) is a nonperturbative impurity solver for steady-state nonequilibrium dynamical mean-field theory (DMFT). It replaces the physical hybridization function by that of a small auxiliary open quantum system, whose parameters are fitted to reproduce it, and then solves the resulting many-body problem essentially exactly. AMEA's accuracy is thus limited by the quality of this fit: since the fastest-varying part of the hybridization is set by the distribution function, whose Fermi edges sharpen at low temperature, the fit tends to overestimate the temperature, especially for weak reservoir coupling. For the auxiliary hybridization itself, however, AMEA's self-energy is essentially exact. We correct for the fit error by estimating, via an approximate solver applicable to arbitrary hybridization functions, the ratio between the self-energies of the physical and auxiliary hybridizations, and using it to extend AMEA's self-energy from the auxiliary to the physical case. This fit-error correction becomes exact whenever either the fit or the approximate solver is exact. We benchmark the scheme using the nonequilibrium Kajueter-Kotliar iterated perturbation theory (IPT), generalized here to spin-polarized solutions, as the approximate solver; other approximate solvers, such as the noncrossing approximation, can be used as well. Applied to an Anderson impurity model with a spin-polarized bath, and within DMFT to a Hubbard layer weakly coupled to two metallic reservoirs, the correction largely removes the spurious heating from the AMEA fit and from insufficient thermalization by the reservoirs, while preserving the correct description of the Mott insulator, which IPT alone cannot fully recover.

Abstract: We investigate magnetic correlations in the two-leg Kondo-Hubbard ladder using the density-matrix renormalization group method. We focus on a parameter regime in which the noninteracting electronic system has a narrow indirect band gap. In this regime, there is no Fermi surface, and the conventional Fermi-surface-based Ruderman-Kittel-Kasuya-Yosida picture is not directly applicable; magnetic correlations between localized spins become weaker as the noninteracting band gap increases. We find that a moderate on-site Coulomb interaction enhances the antiferromagnetic correlations between localized spins. This enhancement, which is also seen in the staggered magnetization induced by a pinning field at the edges, occurs for both ferromagnetic and antiferromagnetic Kondo couplings. Our results show that electron-electron interactions can enhance magnetic correlations between localized spins even when the underlying noninteracting electronic structure is gapped, suggesting an interaction-driven route to magnetic instabilities in narrow-gap Kondo-lattice systems.

Abstract: Collective ordering in correlated electron systems emerges from microscopic dynamics, yet the evolution of observable textures need not admit a closed, memoryless description. We introduce a graph-recurrent neural network (GRNN) framework that learns such emergent dynamics directly from trajectories without specifying microscopic equations of motion. The framework integrates gated recurrent memory with graph message passing: local hidden states encode information beyond the instantaneous observables, while spatial coupling allows this information to propagate between sites. The observable and hidden fields evolve jointly through a shared local rule, providing a scalable representation of dynamics with spatial interactions and temporal memory. We demonstrate this framework on the emergent dynamics of charge-density-wave order in the semiclassical Holstein model, successfully reproducing temporal and spatial correlations and dynamical scaling during domain coarsening. By learning finite-time evolution directly, the GRNN offers a route to efficient, data-driven descriptions of collective dynamics with unresolved microscopic degrees of freedom.

Abstract: The sign problem poses a fundamental barrier to numerically exact quantum Monte Carlo simulations, severely hindering our understanding of quantum many-body physics across disciplines ranging from condensed matter to particle and nuclear physics. Geometrical frustration makes this barrier especially acute for interacting bosons. Here, we develop a bosonic auxiliary-field quantum Monte Carlo (BAFQMC) method and rigorously prove that time-reversal symmetry and reflection positivity each provide a general sufficient condition for sign-problem-free simulations. Crucially, under either criterion, BAFQMC evades the sign problem even on frustrated lattices where conventional world-line and stochastic-series-expansion methods fail. Benchmarking our approach on a two-flavor Bose-Hubbard model on the triangular lattice yields excellent quantitative agreement with finite-temperature exact diagonalization. By establishing unifying principles to solve the sign problem in BAFQMC, our work substantially expands the reach of unbiased, numerically exact simulations for frustrated bosonic matter.

Abstract: We compare relative Abelian BF torsion with the Alexander-graded Euler characteristic of knot Floer homology. A meridional annulus boundary condition identifies the normalized relative torsion with the Alexander polynomial. One exact prime-cyclotomic torsion value recovers all its coefficients under a sharp genus bound, compatibly with connected-sum gluing and branched-cover formulas. We compute interface contractions and obstructions to scalar comparisons, including the pairing defect of two-sector reduction for the degree-zero torus algebra. A six-generator dg resolution yields an equivalence of perfect-module categories preserving derived pairings and admits a classical one-dimensional BV-BFV realization. In the fixed nonresonant polynomial quantum BFV sector, over characteristic zero with $\hbar$ inverted, both states and Weyl observables reduce to a vacuum field; a Hochschild invariant obstructs a Morita equivalence with the degree-zero torus algebra. These results do not establish a full geometric BV-BFV/Floer equivalence.

Abstract: We first prove a sharp Lieb-Thirring inequality with semi-classical constant and gradient correction in one dimension. Moreover, we also characterize the optimizers of these inequalities. In particular, the optimizing density profiles form a two-dimensional manifold, which we call here the Poincaré density manifold.

More precisely, we first obtain an explicit and non-asymptotic bound on the global and local error made by the TFW approximation. These bounds are controlled by the distance of the normalized density profile from the Poincaré density manifold times the number of particles. In particular, these bounds show that the TFW approximation is exact on the Poincaré manifold. We then show that, in the periodic setting, the distance between the Kohn-Sham potential and the inverse TFW potential in the natural dual Sobolev space can also be controlled by explicit density dependent constants times the distance to the Poincaré manifold. As a corollary, we prove that for a fixed density profile, the Kohn-Sham potential converges towards the inverse Thomas--Fermi potential in the high-density limit $N \rightarrow \infty$ with asymptotic rate of order $N^{-2}$. This rate is shown to be optimal.

Abstract: We study the symmetry reduction of optimal control problems using Dirac structures. The first-order conditions of the Pontryagin Maximum Principle are written as an implicit Dirac system on the Pontryagin bundle. For a problem invariant under a Lie group, we reduce both the Lagrange-Pontryagin variational principle and the underlying Dirac structure, and prove that they yield the same reduced equations, which we identify with the Hamiltonian equations on the dual of the Atiyah algebroid. And we determine also the values of the system's key characteristics on these flat trajectories. Next, in the Appendix, we consider some additional related results for a more general model of the system.

Abstract: We show that the concept of particles and antiparticles is related to the Weyl-Heisenberg group $W\!H_3$. Its generators are the position operators $\hat x^a$, the momentum operators $\hat p_a$, and the central element $Q_{\sf{v}}$, which defines a subgroup U(1)$_{\sf v}$ of $W\!H_3$. We show that the wave functions have a charge relative to the group U(1)$_{\sf{v}}$ equal to either $q_+=1/\hbar$ (particle $ψ_+$) or $q_-=-1/\hbar$ (antiparticle $ψ_-$). We call these eigenvalues of the operator $Q_{\sf{v}}$ quantum charges: $Q_{\sf{v}}ψ_\pm^{}=q_\pm^{}ψ_\pm^{}$. The wave functions $ψ_+$ and $ψ_-$ belong to the Hilbert spaces $\mathcal{H}_+$ and $\mathcal{H}_-$, on which unitarily inequivalent representations $π_\hbar$ and $π_{-\hbar}$ of the Weyl-Heisenberg group $W\!H_3$ are defined. These representations are related to each other by the antilinear charge conjugation operator $\hat C$, which maps $\mathcal{H}_+, q_+$ to $\mathcal{H}_-, q_-$. Charges $q_\pm^{}$ are intrinsic characteristics of the wave functions $ψ_\pm^{}$, similar to spin and electric charge. The wave function possesses one of these charges, $q_+$ or $q_-$, prior to the specification of any equations. We show that negative probabilities/energies and the Klein paradox in Klein-Gordon and Dirac theories are consequences of the violation of U(1)$_{\sf v}$-invariance in these theories. Restoring this invariance separates particle and antiparticles and eliminates all paradoxes at the level of first-quantized relativistic theories.

</details>

Abstract: We consider boundary conditions of two-dimensional Dirac fermions which preserve a non-anomalous continuous symmetry which acts differently on the left- and right-movers. Such boundaries are equivalent to topological defects of Weyl fermions preserving the symmetry. We give a general construction of such defects for conformally-embedded symmetries using Chern-Simons theory. They always exist and can be realised as half-space gauging defects for some finite categorical symmetry. Using this technology, we compute the twist lines to which fermions become attached upon scattering through the defect, the boundary central charge of the corresponding folded boundary state, and its mod-2 valued SPT class. As applications, we first consider abelian symmetries and find agreement with known results in the literature. We then study a novel example where an $SU(2) \times SU(4) \times U(1)^2$ symmetry is preserved. In contrast to the abelian case, the twist lines are intrinsically non-invertible and belong to either a $\text{TY}(\mathbb{Z}_3 \times \mathbb{Z}_3)$ or $\text{Rep}(\mathbb{S}_3) \times \mathbb{Z}_3$ symmetry depending on whether scattering is to the left or the right.

Abstract: We give an explicit reconstruction of subleading collinear Yang--Mills amplitudes from one-graviton amplitudes in Einstein--Yang--Mills theory and its string corrections. Abstract: Holographic complexity admits a broad family of bulk observables, yet their relation to boundary state-preparation costs remains unclear. We propose a local prescription that associates suitable bulk observables with quadratic cost geometries on families of states prepared by Euclidean sources. Building on the holographic transport of bulk Hamiltonian flows to Euclidean preparation sources, we use the Hessian of the transported generator at a stationary reference state to define a candidate cost metric. When this Hessian is positive definite, the minimised preparation energy reproduces the change in the bulk observable to quadratic order. We characterise the resulting preparation penalties relative to the Fubini-Study metric and compute them for the maximal volume in scalar perturbations of AdS$_3$. At large-conformal dimension, we recover the known proportionality between cost and Fubini-Study metrics. At finite dimension, we obtain positive but generally unequal penalties for source variations that displace the bulk field and its conjugate momentum, breaking this proportionality even for a single scalar operator. We further study a deformation of volume by quadratic scalar and gradient terms in the large-dimension regime, determining how the couplings modify the penalties and constrain positivity. These additions selectively change field-displacement costs, with the gradient term introducing mode dependence. Our results provide an explicit local connection between the choice of bulk observables and the assignment of boundary preparation penalties.

Abstract: The Euclidean saddles of 3D Einstein gravity with positive cosmological constant are spherical three-manifolds. When one adds a heavy observer, the backreacted saddles are spherical cone manifolds, with a conical singularity along the observer worldline. Our construction relies on a careful analytic continuation of the product of two Virasoro TQFT partition functions. We test the proposal by showing that the exact partition functions exponentiate in the semiclassical limit to the spherical volume, in agreement with the gravitational on-shell action. This leads to a spherical volume conjecture for the product of continued Virasoro TQFT amplitudes. As further evidence, we relate the theta and tetrahedron graph amplitudes to the two-point function and out-of-time-ordered four-point function of operators on a massive observer's worldline. In appropriate limits, we recover the static patch scalar two-point function with finite observer backreaction and the semiclassical effects of observer recoil. In addition, we analyze examples with knotted worldlines and reproduce their spherical volumes. We also apply our prescription to Seifert manifolds, which are off-shell in AdS$_3$ gravity but admit dS$_3$ saddles, and find agreement with their spherical volumes semiclassically. Finally, using Dehn surgery on torus knots and links, we obtain partition functions on smooth spherical three-manifolds.

In general, timelike or null ambient fluids will cross a null hypersurface and lead to an open Carrollian system. On a null foliation, the sources correspond to transport between null leaves. We then show that there exists a decoupling limit of a timelike ambient fluid which gives rise to an autonomous Carrollian fluid with spontaneously broken Carroll boosts and no source terms. This decoupling limit corresponds to an infinite boost while keeping the enthalpy finite and nonzero, which leads to a divergent ambient energy-momentum tensor. Nevertheless, the induced Carrollian responses do not see the divergent term and remain finite, and an independent Carroll boost Goldstone emerges from a subleading component of the ambient fluid velocity in this limit.

Abstract: We study Maxwell-Chern-Simons theory on AdS$_3$ coupled to $N$ complex scalars or Dirac fermions, including sextic and Gross-Neveu interactions, respectively. We determine the corresponding phase diagrams, and we resum matter insertions to obtain the photon propagator at leading order in large $N$, exactly in $α$ and $κ$. In the fermionic phases, the current persists for nonzero effective Chern-Simons level, with helicity determined by the induced level shift. Abstract: We show that the ordinary expansion of EFT corrections to individual QNM frequencies breaks down at an exceptional QNM, and that these corrections become enhanced for near-exceptional QNMs. We give a brief argument for why this should generally happen, before numerically demonstrating this behaviour by calculating the $α'^3$ corrections to QNMs of equal-spin Myers--Perry-AdS$_5$ black holes. Motivation: 黑洞 S 矩阵在关联散射场或弦论散射振幅时会出现奇异性，尤其当 I 上两点被体空间零测地线连接时。研究这些光锥奇异性及其在有限弦长下的消除机制，有助于理解黑洞周围的弦论散射和准正规模行为。

Abstract: The S matrix on an asymptotically-flat Schwarzschild black hole is studied as a function of position on $\mathcal{I}$. We derive the lightcone singularities that arise in the S matrix whenever the two points on $\mathcal{I}$ are connected by a bulk null geodesic. This includes geodesics orbiting near the photon sphere for a long time, whose kinematics determine the associated lightcone singularities. By turning on a finite string length $\ell_s$, worldsheet corrections are shown to shift the lightcone singularities into the complex time plane, resolving the singularities at real times. In particular, we derive the leading stringy resolution of the photon sphere singularities. We also compute stringy corrections to the eikonal quasinormal mode spectrum of Schwarzschild, and discuss the kinematics of strings orbiting the photon sphere.

</details>

Abstract: Seven-dimensional supergravity poses a uniquely intricate supersymmetry problem, combining topologically massive higher-form gauge dynamics, distinct Clifford algebra properties, and rich geometric structures. We first establish the complete formulation of the maximal Cartan-gauged theory in terms of the gauge-invariant higher-form flux, including both the bosonic action and supersymmetry variations. This formulation, which also makes the smooth ungauged limit manifest, is achieved by requiring a consistent truncation to the minimal Cartan-gauged theory in the equal-charge limit. We construct the complete bilinear system for a timelike Killing vector, where additional internal orientation of the symplectic-Majorana spinor arises already in the scalar sector, uniquely among pure minimal ungauged theories. In the equal-charge sector, we decouple the algebraic constraints into SU$(3)$-isotypic components, and analyse the differential bilinear relations, determining the permitted dilaton gradient, fluxes, fibration twist and intrinsic torsion of the base. We prove that with gauging and active Cartan flux, the complex scalar bilinear vanishes identically. We also construct a Killing gauge for the higher-form potential compatible with the self-duality condition. We show that with gauging but no active Cartan flux, the Killing vector also acts as a generator of the gauged R-symmetry, and the base structure is enlarged from SU$(3)$ to U$(3)$. We find that BPS-flow solutions realize this setting. With supersymmetry, we isolate a single independent component of the 3-form equation across an exhaustive four-class partition for both theories within this setup. Applied to known and proposed solutions in both theories, this classification exposes unexplored sectors and provides an accessible toolbox for constructing and testing solutions and developing new geometric and holographic applications.

Abstract: We study the exact Onsager algebra of free lattice fermions in staggered, Wilson, and overlap incarnations, working in both Hamiltonian and Euclidean formulations in four spacetime dimensions. In each case, we show that the exact lattice algebra emanates the full chiral symmetry of the low-energy theory: if there are $N$ massless Dirac fermions in the continuum, the Onsager algebra reduces to a $U(2N)$ chiral symmetry. The details of which generators are quantised or ultra-local differ between formulations. There is also a trade-off between the locality of generators and the maximum compact subgroup of the Onsager algebra.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 广义 Born–Infeld 与对数模型领头阶相互作用一致，但前者压强饱和而后者无界增长；二者的精确生成元分别为二次与非多项式形式，后者含 Lambert W_{-1}。重建显式量化了领头阶匹配之外的非线性自由度，且同一剖面在两个表述下一致，流局域对易于根号 T\bar T 并保持共同 Lax 形式。

Conclusion: 精确重建表明，领头阶 T\bar T 匹配并不唯一决定强场物理，因果可积模型在领头阶一致下仍保留由剖面函数刻画的丰富非线性自由度；分离应力尺度与迹比是把握该自由度的有效手段。

Abstract: The same leading $T\bar T$ interaction can coexist with sharply different strong-field responses, even within causal, classically integrable sigma models. We reconstruct exact autonomous stress-tensor generators for prescribed one-scale Courant--Hilbert families in two dimensions. Separating the invariant stress scale from a signed trace ratio reduces reconstruction to one scalar inversion on each invertible physical branch. The resulting profile determines both the deformation law and the relation between local energy density and pressure. For generalized Born--Infeld and logarithmic models, the leading interactions coincide, yet pressure saturates in the former and grows without bound in the latter. Their exact generators are respectively quadratic and nonpolynomial, the latter involving Lambert $W_{-1}$. A regular Legendre transformation gives the same profile in the auxiliary potential formulation. The flows commute locally with root-$T\bar T$ within a common smooth branch and retain a common classical Lax form. Exact reconstruction thus quantifies the nonlinear freedom left by leading-order matching.

</details>

Abstract: We develop a new framework for evaluating higher-loop integrals for scalar fields on $S^D$. Starting from a Schwinger-parametric form for general higher-loop integrals on the sphere, it combines total-derivative identities with edge-contraction relations into a coupled linear system. To illustrate our method, we solve for all vacuum graphs through two loops. This includes, in particular, the two-loop three-melon, for which we construct the analytic solution for general $D$ and obtain explicit dimensionally regularized results at all integer dimensions.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文研究由 A_3 根格与权格沿 Z_4 判别群粘合得到的 Narain 共形场论，重点发展其物理诠释及其与三维拓扑场论的联系。作者证明 22 个偶自对偶粘合码都是同一个阿贝尔 Chern-Simons 理论的拓扑边界条件，其可逆对称性将边界组织为自由、混合、挠三个轨道，并系统研究各理论间的配分函数、谱与流代数的变化，最终证明有限 Siegel-Weil 恒等式并分析 handlebody 求和的物理含义。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 伴随论文对两类双曲对给出了 22 个偶自对偶粘合码（对应 c=\bar c=6 的 7 个不等价理论），但缺乏对这些理论的物理诠释及其拓扑场论关联的系统理解。作者希望通过将粘合码解释为同一阿贝尔 Chern-Simons 理论的边界条件，澄清理论间的对称性组织关系、配分（gauging）关系，并利用 handlebody 求和理解系综平均的物理来源。

Method: 作者将所有 22 个码确立为单个阿贝尔 Chern-Simons 理论的拓扑边界条件；利用包含分数 B-场平移的可逆对称性把边界分成自由、混合、挠三个轨道；系统枚举七个理论间所有阶数不超过四的配分（Z_2、Z_4、Z_2×Z_2），并给出显式的扭曲配分函数及其对谱和流代数的影响；随后对亏格一的 handlebody 求 Chern-Simons 配分函数之和，证明任意多个对都成立的有限 Siegel-Weil 恒等式，并考察 handlebody 和作为码边界态组合的性质。

Conclusion: 该研究表明粘合码赋予的 Narain 理论可通过单一阿贝尔 Chern-Simons 理论的拓扑边界条件获得统一的物理诠释，可逆对称性与配分操作完整刻画了七个不等价理论之间的关系。有限 Siegel-Weil 恒等式从拓扑场论角度解释了系综平均：两对时 handlebody 和给出自由理论的平均，需补充挠环面贡献才能覆盖全部理论；三对及以上时 handlebody 和不再对应码边界态的正组合，揭示该结构对对数目的依赖。

Abstract: In a companion paper arXiv:2610.26610, we classified the Narain conformal field theories obtained by gluing copies of the $A_3$ root and weight lattices along their $\mathbb{Z}_4$ discriminant group. For two hyperbolic pairs, this gives twenty-two even self-dual gluing codes, which fall into seven inequivalent theories at $c=\bar c=6$. In this paper, we develop the physical interpretation of this family and its connections to three-dimensional topological field theory. We show that all twenty-two codes are topological boundary conditions of a single abelian Chern-Simons theory. Its invertible symmetries, which include fractional $B$-field shifts, organize the boundaries into three orbits: free, mixed and torsion. Boundaries in different orbits are related only by gauging. We determine all gaugings of order at most four between the seven theories, by $\mathbb{Z}_2$, $\mathbb{Z}_4$ and $\mathbb{Z}_2\times\mathbb{Z}_2$, with explicit twisted partition functions, and follow their effect on the spectrum and the current algebra. We then sum the Chern-Simons partition function over handlebodies at genus one and prove a finite Siegel-Weil identity, valid for any number of pairs. For two pairs, the handlebody sum is the uniform average over the free theories. Recovering the average over all twenty-two theories requires one additional contribution, a solid torus with the torsion lines along its core. For three or more pairs, we find that the handlebody sum is not a positive combination of code boundary states.

</details>

Abstract: We construct a family of integrable 2d sigma models through 4d Chern-Simons theory and show that their classical point-particle reduction corresponds to a Hitchin system. The integrable Hitchin quantisation then provides a natural candidate Hamiltonian for the one-loop reduced dynamics of the sigma models, which coincides with the Perelman operator in all cases examined. This allows us to study the corresponding spectral problems using 4d $\mathcal{N}=2$ gauge theory and 2d conformal field theory methods. For the sausage model, we derive exact quantisation conditions and compute exponentially small corrections essential for the agreement with numerical finite-volume results.

*Byoungjoon Ahn,Robert de Mello Koch,Vatsal Garg,Antal Jevicki,Junggi Yoon*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有双局域全息研究大多局限于建立在Fock真空上的扇区，而色群G与双局域代数在微观振子Fock空间上的对偶作用表明存在更多由群阿贝尔化刻画的扇区。作者希望超越Fock真空，系统刻画这些额外扇区及其物理实现与可观测量。

Method: 作者运用Howe对偶：当微观振子Fock空间承载紧色群G与双局域代数的重数自由对偶作用时，投影到导出子群[G,G]恰好挑选Fock空间中出现的Gab=G/[G,G]的字符。对O(N)、U(N)、Sp(N)分别考察其阿贝尔化（Z2、U(1)、平凡群），并结合Bose/Fermi统计与模式数确定实际实现的扇区。继而构造生成这些扇区的epsilon与行列式背景及其有限N约束和Casimir不变量，并利用群积分投影算子推导O(N)与U(N)理论的扇区分辨字符，再用辅助振子求解约束。最后在R×S2上的自由向量模型中计算重子型原初算符的能量、简并度与自旋内容。

Conclusion: 双局域全息的希尔伯特空间并非仅由Fock真空扇区构成，色群的阿贝尔化结构会诱导演生出由epsilon张量与行列式算子背景生成的新扇区。该方法给出有限N下精确且按扇区分解的字符与谱数据，揭示了玻色物质中的费米海结构与重子型原初算符的物理特征，为超越Fock真空的双局域全息提供了系统框架。

Abstract: We discuss the finite-$N$ Hilbert space of bilocal operators that underlies bilocal holography of vector models, beyond the sector built on the Fock vacuum. If the microscopic oscillator Fock space carries a multiplicity-free dual action of a compact color group $G$ and a bilocal algebra, Howe duality implies that projection to the derived subgroup $[G,G]$ selects precisely the characters of the abelianization $G^{\rm ab}=G/[G,G]$ that occur in the Fock space. Thus $O(N)/SO(N)=\mathbb Z_2$ gives two sectors, $U(N)/SU(N)=U(1)$ gives integer determinant-charge sectors, while $Sp(N)$ is perfect and gives no additional sector; Bose/Fermi statistics and the number of modes determine which sectors are actually realized. We construct the epsilon and determinant backgrounds that generate these sectors, together with their finite-$N$ constraints and Casimirs, and find that the two bosonic $O(N)$ sectors are not distinguished by any Casimir. Using group-integral projectors, we derive explicit sector-resolved characters for the $O(N)$ and $U(N)$ theories. Around each new background we solve the constraints with auxiliary oscillators, which form a Fermi sea for bosonic matter. In the free vector model on $\mathbb R\times S^2$ the new sectors consist of baryonic primaries; we determine their energies, degeneracies and spin content exactly at finite $N$, together with their lowest energy at fixed angular momentum.

</details>

Abstract: MHV form factors of the chiral stress-tensor multiplet in planar $\mathcal N=4$ super Yang-Mills theory are dual to periodic null Wilson loops. We propose a periodic $\bar q$ equation for the MHV and higher-helicity sectors at vanishing fermionic monodromy. The equation relates kinematic derivatives of $\mathrm N^k\mathrm{MHV}$ form factors to collinear integrals of $\mathrm N^{k+1}\mathrm{MHV}$ form factors with one additional external particle, providing a recursive relation in loop order. In periodic momentum supertwistors, it has the same collinear-integral form as the two dotted components of the amplitude $\bar Q$ equation, with coefficient $Γ_{\rm cusp}/4$, despite the absence of ordinary dual conformal invariance at fixed operator momentum. For parity-even scalar MHV remainders, combining the equation with its parity image supplies all directions of the kinematic differential.

Further two-loop MHV tests give numerical agreement with derivatives of the known four-point remainder at a fixed kinematic configuration, and for five points, all six chiral derivatives agree exactly at symbol level for a one-parameter family of kinematics. The structure of the collinear source further gives all-loop predictions for MHV last entries, consistent with known results and form a space of dimension $n(2n-5)$. The periodic Wilson-loop picture also motivates an all-loop derivation. Using the form-factor OPE, we extract the one-loop correction to the fermion energy from the logarithmic source and recover its vanishing at zero momentum.

Conclusion: 该结果为黑洞奇点化解提供了全息信号，并阐明了正则黑洞内部信息如何编码在边界关联函数中。

Abstract: The black hole singularity is hidden behind its horizon, and it is difficult to determine whether it is resolved by quantum gravity or stringy effects. For an AdS-Schwarzschild black hole, it is known that the two-point function of the dual conformal field theory has a singularity associated with a null geodesic that bounces off the black hole singularity. In this paper, we investigate how the resolution of the black hole singularity is encoded in the dual conformal field theory by considering regular AdS black holes as regularized geometries. Using WKB analysis, we compute the quasinormal modes of regular AdS black holes and read off the dual two-point functions from the modes. We find that the singularity corresponding to the bouncing null geodesic for singular black holes is replaced by a smooth bump associated with a complex, rather than real, null geodesic. The location of the complex geodesic singularity is supported by analyzing the structure of the operator product expansion for the two-point function. Our results provide a holographic signature of black hole singularity resolution and clarify how information about the regular interior is encoded in boundary correlation functions.

</details>

Conclusion: 该高精度结果为进一步精确的超越广义相对论的引力波检验提供了关键要素，证明了EFT方法在标量-张量理论高阶后牛顿阶计算中的可行性与有效性。

Abstract: We compute the static contribution to the conservative two-body potential in massless scalar-tensor theories through fifth post-Newtonian (5PN) order, with the strong-field scalar responses of the compact objects encoded in scalar-dependent masses. Within post-Newtonian effective field theory (EFT), evaluating the 1PN-5PN static terms requires one- through five-loop massless two-point Feynman integrals. Although the additional scalar modifies both source couplings and integrand numerators, the required scalar master topologies remain those of the corresponding static problem in general relativity. Moreover, independent reflection symmetries of the two static scalar sectors extend the odd-PN factorization mechanism to the present theory, so that no genuinely new five-loop master integrals are required. Our results reproduce the known static interaction through 2PN, provide an independent EFT determination at 3PN, and extend the explicit potential to 4PN and 5PN. We verify the general-relativity limit and body-exchange symmetry; regulator poles and residual transcendental terms cancel in the complete result, leaving rational coefficients. These high-precision results provide ingredients for increasingly precise gravitational-wave tests of gravity beyond general relativity.

</details>

Abstract: We study the Euclidean path integral around magnetically charged lukewarm black holes in four-dimensional de Sitter space. In the small-mass limit, certain localized eigenmodes can be effectively viewed as describing the observer's motion and have the expected eigenvalues, allowing us to reconstruct the worldline action. We further identify these modes with translational zero modes of an extremal charged black hole in asymptotically flat spacetime, lifted by de Sitter curvature.

Abstract: We consider $\mathcal{N}=4$ supersymmetric Yang--Mills theory with gauge group SU$(N)$, which admits families of conformal surface defects defined by singular boundary conditions for scalar fields that completely break supersymmetry. We also construct embeddings of both D5- and D7-branes describing defect renormalisation group (RG) flows, for which the ultraviolet and infrared fixed points are both dual to the same D$p$-brane, with different values of a parameter. Our embeddings dual to RG flows are mostly constructed numerically, but we find a regime in which we can derive approximate analytic embeddings using a controlled perturbative expansion. We demonstrate that the $b$-theorem is satisfied by all of the RG flows that we study.

Abstract: Hidden zeros of tree-level string amplitudes of colored scalars lie on loci specified by integer-valued dimensionless momentum contractions arranged in rectangles on the kinematic mesh. We study the same kinematic loci for open-string amplitudes with gluons, fermions and higher-spin states at arbitrary finite mass levels. Disk monodromy relations yield $\textit{zero-locus factorization}$: at regular points where the monodromy system is invertible, the amplitude is a weighted sum of physical three-point amplitudes sewn to complete on-shell $(n-1)$-point amplitudes. Hidden zeros follow when the external wavefunctions annihilate all contributing three-point couplings. In the low-energy limit, the massless zero-integer branch recovers the corresponding field-theory factorization. For closed strings, including gravitons, persistent cubic zeros in both chiral sectors remove the disk poles, allowing the Kawai--Lewellen--Tye momentum-kernel zero to imply amplitude zeros. The operator product expansions of external vertices give a common worldsheet origin for these conditions across spins and mass levels.
